People and mountains exhibit the impact in 101:4-5; the paired heavy and light balances in 101:6 and 101:8 activate the allocative branch. The noun's role is therefore a hinge between world-level disruption and person-level sorting. I infer that the same named event governs both phases; sequence and the `فَأَمَّا` division supply the evidence but not the causal unity. The ayah comes to ask about an event whose force is simultaneously destructive and discriminating.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_000473/B002","root_000473/B003","root_000473/B004","root_001219/B001","root_001219/B006","root_001219/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6264ed43c1b13f52e729","text":"1. Knowledge struck into insufficiency. Evidence: د ر ي B001, knowing and causing another to know; ق ر ع B006, a striking warning/rebuke. Structural cue: أدراك is inserted between two ما-elements and the repeated قارعة. Mechanism: the address is made to know by being checked at the limit of knowing. Abductive move: I supply the paradox that making-known can operate by showing inadequacy. Reading change: the ayah becomes an epistemic blow: knowledge begins where mastery fails. 2. Aiming at a hidden event. Evidence: د ر ي B002, aiming at or setting upon something; د ر ي B003, stealth/concealment in hunting; ق ر ع B010, open place; ن و ر B005 later, beacon/landmark. Structural cue: the addressed \"you\" is oriented toward an event that remains undefined until 101:11. Mechanism: the question points the mind like a hunter toward a concealed target, with later fire as the revealed marker. Abductive move: I infer that أدراك can activate directed pursuit, not only cognition. Reading change: the ayah reads as guided orientation toward a hidden endpoint. 3. Sharpness against the head-core. Evidence: د ر ي B004, sharp horn/comb-like implement; ق ر ع B001, striking; ء م م B003 later, head-core and wound reaching it. Structural cue: striking terminology surrounds a knowledge-verb whose branch includes pointed instruments, later answered by an \"umm/head-core\" field. Mechanism: knowing is figured as an impact or penetration into the guarded center. Abductive move: I supply the bodily analogy linking mental knowing to head-core contact. Reading change: أدراك becomes not soft explanation but pointed, forceful disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7e769d0065676540da17","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:3:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hitap, dikkati belirsiz olayın kendisinden onun sonuçlarına doğru yöneltir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_96b7f69e1b40291aa119","text":"1. **Knowledge caused through a sequence rather than a definition.** `د ر ي B001` includes both knowing and causing another to know. After the causative `أَدْرَىٰكَ`, 101:4-5 provide two transformations and 101:6-8 provide opposed scale states. Those scenes serve as the means by which awareness is produced. The packet supplies their succession but not the claim that each scene is instructional; that causal pedagogy is my abductive move. The question reads as the opening of a staged act of making known, not as a claim that the event is inaccessible to all description.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b09c9cee6d7c62654ab2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:3:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bildirme, doğrudan tanım vermek yerine dönüşüm ve tartı sahnelerini art arda gösteren aşamalı bir öğretme süreci olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_001219/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b256904fed8cdb936093","text":"The paired transformations in 101:4-5 showed that `د ر ي B001` causes knowledge by displaying effects, not by supplying a lexical definition. More surprisingly, the matched heavy and light clauses in 101:6 and 101:8 corrected an impact-only reading of `ٱلْقَارِعَةُ`: `ق ر ع B004` becomes visible as a sorting echo because the same event that destabilizes the world also differentiates persons.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_001219/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bdaaad3db5e3d846631f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:3:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]],[\"101:3:4\",\"root_001219\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Daha sonra yinelenen bildirme formülü, bilginin denetim altına alma sınırını gösteren iki aşamalı zihinsel darbenin ilkini kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001","root_001219/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cf2d63869dc8214da3b9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:3:4\",\"root_001219\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Felaket adı burada hem dünyayı sarsan darbeyi hem de kişileri ayıran hüküm sürecini birleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e1fb657f23c247e6bdf2","text":"2. **Attention aimed at consequences.** Exploratorily, `د ر ي B002` supplies aiming at and setting upon something. The repeated `مَا ... مَا ٱلْقَارِعَةُ` fixes attention on one target, after which the text narrows from universal disturbance to individual balances. This branch makes `أَدْرَىٰكَ` an attentional vector directing the addressee toward consequences. The packet does not identify epistemic direction with the branch's raid-oriented motion; that analogy is the weak abductive link.
